In December 2021, the National Healthcare Security Administration ( 國 家             於2021年12月 ， 國 家 醫 療 保 障 局 與 國 家 中
醫 療 保 障 局 ) and the National Administration of Traditional Chinese                  醫 藥 管 理 局 聯 合 發 佈《關 於 醫 保 支 持 中 醫 藥
Medicine ( 國 家 中 醫 藥 管 理 局 ) jointly issued the Guidance on Supporting              傳 承 創 新 發 展 的 指 導 意 見》， 當 中 提 出 (i) 支
the Inheritance, Innovation and Development of TCM Healthcare Services              持「互 聯 網 +」中 醫 藥 發 展 並 納 入 醫 保 計 劃 ；

  Non-HKFRS Measures                                                       非香港財務報告準則計量

  To supplement the financial information, which is presented in           為補充按照香港財務報告準則會計準則呈
  accordance with HKFRS Accounting Standards, the Company also             列的財務資料 ， 本公司亦提供經調整溢利

  (non-HKFRS measure) as non-HKFRS measures, which is unaudited in         舊 及 攤 銷 前 盈 利（非 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 計 量）

  Accounting Standards. The Company believes that the non-HKFRS            審核性質 ， 並非香港財務報告準則會計準
  measures (i) facilitate period-to-period comparisons of operating        則規定或根據香港財務報告準則會計準則
  performance by eliminating potential impacts of items that its           呈列 。 本公司認為 ， 非香港財務報告準則
  management does not consider to be indicative of its operating           計 量 (i) 消 除 管 理 層 認 為 並 非 其 營 運 表 現 指 標
  performance; and (ii) provide useful information to investors in         項目的潛在影響 ， 有助比較同比營運表現；
  understanding and evaluating its results of consolidated statements of   及 (ii) 如 同 協 助 其 管 理 層 般 ， 為 投 資 者 提 供
  profit or loss and other comprehensive income in the same manner as      有用資料 ， 以了解及評估其綜合損益及其
  they helped its management. However, its presentation of adjusted net    他全面收益表的業績 。 然而 ， 本集團呈列
  profit (non-HKFRS measure) and EBITDA (non-HKFRS measure) may not        的 經 調 整 溢 利 淨 額（非 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 計
  be comparable to similarly titled measures presented by other            量）及 稅 息 折 舊 及 攤 銷 前 盈 利（非 香 港 財 務
  companies as they do not have a standardized meaning. The application    報 告 準 則 計 量）不 可 與 其 他 公 司 所 呈 列 類 似
  of the non-HKFRS measures has limitations as an analytical tool, and     名稱的計量作比較 ， 因為其並無標準意義 。
  the Shareholders and investors should not consider them in isolation     作為分析工具 ， 應用非香港財務報告準則
  from, or as substitutes for analysis of, its results of operations or    計量有其限制 ， 股東及投資者不應對其單
  financial condition as reported under HKFRS Accounting Standards.        獨考慮 ， 或以其代替本集團根據香港財務

Foreign Currency Risk                                                         外匯風險

The Group has transactional currency exposures. Such exposures arise          本集團有交易貨幣風險 。 有關風險來自於
from the use of financial instruments denominated in USD or HKD to            使用以美元或港元計值的金融工具為其於
finance its operations in the PRC and the fact that the repayment of          中國的業務提供資金 ， 但該等以美元計值
those USD-denominated or HKD-denominated financial instruments is             或以港元計值的金融工具乃根據其於中國
based on the RMB-denominated assets generated by its PRC                      的業務所產生以人民幣計值的資產進行還款 。
operations. The Group has no foreign currency hedging policy. However,        本集團並無外匯對沖政策 。 然而 ， 其管理
its management monitors foreign exchange exposures and will consider          層會監控外匯風險 ， 並將在未來有需要時
appropriate hedging measures in the future should the need arise.             考慮採取適當的對沖措施 。

  COMPLIANCE WITH THE CORPORATE                                            遵守企業管治守則
  GOVERNANCE CODE

  The Group is committed to maintaining high standards of corporate        本集團致力維持高水平的企業管治 ， 以維
  governance to safeguard the interests of the Shareholders and to         護股東的權益並改善企業價值及問責性 。
  enhance corporate value and accountability. The Company has adopted      本 公 司 已 採 納 上 市 規 則 附 錄 C1所 載 企 業 管
  the CG Code as set out in Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules as its own    治守則作為其自身的企業管治守則 。
  code of corporate governance.


  The Board considered that the Company has complied with all              董 事 會 認 為 ， 本 公 司 於 截 至2025年6月30日
  applicable code provisions as set out in the CG Code during the six      止六個月已遵守企業管治守則所載的所有
  months ended June 30, 2025, save and except for code provision C.2.1     適用守則條文 ， 惟企業管治守則第二部分
  in part 2 of the CG Code.                                                守 則 條 文 第 C.2.1條 除 外 。


  Pursuant to code provision C.2.1 in part 2 of the CG Code, the roles     根 據 企 業 管 治 守 則 第 二 部 分 守 則 條 文 第 C.2.1
  of chairman and chief executive officer should be separate and should    條 ， 主席及首席執行官的角色應有所區分 ，
  not be performed by the same individual. Mr. Tu is currently serving     不應由一人同時兼任 。 涂先生現任本公司
  as the chairman of the Board as well as the chief executive officer      董事會主席兼首席執行官 。 由於涂先生為
  of the Company. As Mr. Tu is the founder of the Group and has            本集團的創始人 ， 自本集團成立起一直管
  been managing the Group’s business and overall strategic planning        理本集團的業務及整體戰略規劃 ， 故董事
  since its establishment, the Directors consider that vesting the roles   認為 ， 將主席及首席執行官職位授予涂先
  of chairman and chief executive officer in Mr. Tu is beneficial to the   生 ， 有利於本集團的業務前景及管理 ， 確
  business prospects and management of the Group by ensuring               保本集團內的一貫領導 。 因此 ， 董事會認
  consistent leadership within the Group. The Board therefore considers    為 ， 在有關情況下偏離企業管治守則第二
  it is appropriate to deviate from code provision C.2.1 in part 2 of      部 分 守 則 條 文 第 C.2.1條 屬 恰 當 之 舉 。 如 有
  the CG Code in such circumstances. The Board will continue to            需要 ， 經考慮本集團的整體情況 ， 董事會
  review and consider splitting the roles of chairman of the Board and     將繼續檢討並於適當時候考慮分拆本公司
  the chief executive officer of the Company at an appropriate time if     董事會主席及首席執行官的角色 。 儘管有
  necessary, taking into account the circumstances of the Group as a       上述規定 ， 董事會認為管理架構對本集團
  whole. Notwithstanding the foregoing, the Board considers that the       營運有效 ， 且已經建立足夠的制衡機制 。
  management structure is effective for the operation of the Group and
  those adequate checks and balances have been put in place.


  The Company will continue to review and monitor its corporate            本公司將繼續審閱及監查其企業管治常規
  governance practices to ensure compliance with the CG Code.              以確保符合企業管治守則 。

  SHARE INCENTIVE SCHEMES                                                       股份激勵計劃

  Pre-IPO Share Option Plan                                                     首次公開發售前購股權計劃

  The Pre-IPO Share Option Plan was approved and adopted by the                 首 次 公 開 發 售 前 購 股 權 計 劃 於2021年3月31
  Company on March 31, 2021. The purpose of the Pre-IPO Share Option            日獲本公司批准及採納 。 首次公開發售前
  Plan is to promote the success and enhance the value of the Company           購股權計劃的目的乃透過將合資格參與者
  by linking the personal interests of the eligible participants to those of    的個人利益與股東的利益掛鈎 ， 藉著激勵
  the Shareholders and by providing such individuals with an incentive to       該等個人傑出表現 ， 為股東帶來豐厚回報 ，
  generate superior returns to the Shareholders through their outstanding       以促進本公司的成功及提升其價值 。 首次
  performance. The Pre-IPO Share Option Plan is further intended to             公開發售前購股權計劃進一步旨在使本公
  provide flexibility to the Company in its ability to motivate, attract, and   司能靈活激勵 、 吸引及留用董事 、 高級管
  retain the services of directors, senior management, other employees          理層 、 其他僱員及個人提供服務 ， 而本公
  and individuals upon whose judgment, interest, contribution and special       司能否成功經營業務 ， 將主要取決於該等
  effort the successful conduct of the Company’s operation is largely           人士的判斷 、 利益 、 貢獻及特別努力 。 首
  dependent. Further details of the Pre-IPO Share Option Plan are set out       次公開發售前購股權計劃的進一步詳情載
  in the section headed “Statutory and General Information — D. Pre-IPO         於 招 股 章 程 附 錄 四「法 定 及 一 般 資 料 — D. 首
  Share Option Plan” of Appendix IV to the Prospectus.                          次 公 開 發 售 前 購 股 權 計 劃」一 節 。

      to understand the impact of a currency not being exchangeable.             務報表使用者能夠了解貨幣不可兌換

3   OPERATING SEGMENT INFORMATION                                            3   經營分部資料

    The Group is principally engaged in the provision of a variety               本集團主要從事提供各種醫療健康業
    of healthcare businesses, including the provision of healthcare              務 ， 包括提供醫療健康服務和銷售醫
    services and sale of healthcare products.                                    療健康產品 。


    HKFRS 8 Operating Segments requires operating segments to                    香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第8號 經 營 分 部 規
    be identified on the basis of internal reporting about components            定 ， 經營分部按主要經營決策者為分
    of the Group that are regularly reviewed by the chief operating              配資源予各分部及評估其表現而定期
    decision-maker in order to allocate resources to segments and to             審閱的有關本集團組成部分的內部報
    assess their performance. The information reported to the directors          告為基礎而區分 。 為進行資源分配及
    of the Company, who are the chief operating decision-makers, for             表 現 評 估 而 向 本 公 司 董 事（主 要 經 營
    the purpose of resource allocation and assessment of performance             決 策 者）報 告 的 資 料 並 不 包 含 不 連 續
    does not contain discrete operating segment financial information            的經營分部財務資料 ， 且董事審閱本
    and the directors reviewed the financial results of the Group as             集團整體的財務業績 。 因此 ， 並無呈
    a whole. Therefore, no further information about the operating               報有關經營分部的進一步資料 。
    segment is presented.


    Seasonality of operations                                                    營運季節性

    In line with the healthcare industry in the Mainland China, where            本集團大部分收入來自中國內地 ， 及
    the majority of the Group’s revenue is derived from, the Group               與中國內地的醫療健康行業一樣 ， 本
    typically generate a majority of its total revenue in the second half        集團的大部分總收入通常來自下半年 ，
    of the year, during which customers in certain geographic regions            因為在下半年 ， 某些地區的客戶傾向
    tend to receive healthcare solutions and purchase healthcare                 於接受醫療健康解決方案及購買醫療
    products to promote well-being with the weather getting cold.                健康產品 ， 以便在天氣變冷時促進健
    Additionally, customers usually avoid visiting medical institutions          康 。 此外 ， 客戶通常會在春節前後避
    shortly before and after the Chinese New Year. As a result of the            免前往醫療機構 。 因此 ， 上半年的收
    foregoing, the revenue was slightly lower in the first half of the           入略低 。
    year.

  8   INCOME TAX                                                               8   所得稅

      The Group is subject to income tax on an entity basis on profits             本集團須就本集團成員公司所處及經
      arising in or derived from the jurisdictions in which members of the         營所在司法權區產生或賺取的溢利 ，
      Group are domiciled and operate.                                             按實體基準繳納所得稅 。


      Mainland China                                                               中國內地

      Pursuant to the Corporate Income Tax Law of the PRC and the                  根據中國企業所得稅法及相關規定（「企
      respective regulations (the “CIT Law”), the subsidiaries of the              業 所 得 稅 法」）， 本 集 團 於 中 國 內 地 經
      Group which operates in Mainland China are subject to corporate              營 的 附 屬 公 司 須 按 應 課 稅 收 入 的25%
      income tax at a rate of 25% on the taxable income unless those               繳納企業所得稅 ， 惟下文所述稅項減
      are subject to tax exemption set out below.                                  免的情況除外 。


      A subsidiary is qualified as an “High and New Technology                     一 家 附 屬 公 司 被 認 定 為「高 新 技 術 企
      Enterprise” and therefore was entitled to a preferential income tax          業」， 因 此 於2022年 至2024年 享 有15%
      rate of 15% for the years from 2022 to 2024. As at 30 June 2025,             的 優 惠 所 得 稅 稅 率 。 於2025年6月30
      it is in the progress of re-application of “High and New Technology          日 ， 其 正 在 重 新 申 請「高 新 技 術 企 業」
      Enterprise” and expected that it is probable to be recognised                認 定 及 預 期 可 能 會 被 認 定 為「高 新 技
      as “High and New Technology Enterprise”. Thus the subsidiary                 術 企 業」。 因 此 該 附 屬 公 司 於 期 內 按
      calculated the income tax provision as the preferential rate of 15%          15% 的 優 惠 稅 率 計 提 所 得 稅 撥 備 。 此
      in the periods. In addition, a subsidiary qualified for a preferential       外 ， 根據附屬公司所在地區的稅收激
      tax rate of 15% for the period pursuant to the eligibility criteria          勵政策中的資格標準 ， 一家附屬公司
      in the tax incentive policies of the district where the subsidiary is        在 期 內 有 資 格 享 受15% 的 優 惠 稅 率 。
      located.


      Furthermore, certain subsidiaries of the Group in the Mainland               此外 ， 本集團於中國內地的若干附屬
      China are qualified as “Small and Micro Enterprises” and therefore           公 司 被 認 定 為「小 微 企 業」， 因 此 於 期
      were entitled to a preferential income tax rate of 5% to 10% during          內 有 權 享 受5% 至10% 的 優 惠 所 得 稅 稅
      the periods.                                                                 率。


      Hong Kong                                                                    香港

      No provision for Hong Kong profits tax has been made as the                  由於本公司於報告期內並無源自香港
      Company had no assessable profits derived from or earned                     或在香港賺取的應課稅溢利 ， 故並無
      in Hong Kong during the reporting period. The subsidiary                     就香港利得稅作出撥備 。 在香港註冊
      incorporated in Hong Kong is subject to income tax at the rate of            成立的附屬公司須就期內在香港產生
      16.5% on the estimated assessable profits arising in Hong Kong               的 估 計 應 課 稅 溢 利 按16.5% 稅 率 繳 納 所
      during the periods.                                                          得稅 。

8   INCOME TAX (CONTINUED)                                              8   所 得 稅（續）

    Other jurisdictions                                                     其他司法權區

23 FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE                                              23 金 融 工 具 的 公 允 價 值 及
   HIERARCHY OF FINANCIAL                                                    公 允 價 值 層 級（續）
   INSTRUMENTS (CONTINUED)

  The Group’s finance department headed by the finance manager                     本集團財務部由財務經理領導 ， 負責
  is responsible for determining the policies and procedures for                   釐定金融工具公允價值計量的政策和
  the fair value measurement of financial instruments. The finance                 程序 。 財務經理直接向首席財務官及
  manager reports directly to the chief financial officer and the                  審核委員會匯報 。 於各報告日期 ， 財
  audit committee. At each reporting date, the finance department                  務部分析金融工具的價值變動 ， 並釐
  analyses the movements in the values of financial instruments and                定估值所用的主要輸入數據 。 估值經
  determines the major inputs applied in the valuation. The valuation              首席財務官審閱及批准 。
  is reviewed and approved by the chief financial officer.


  The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are included             金融資產及負債的公允價值按自願方
  at the amount at which the instrument could be exchanged in a                    之 間 進 行 即 期 交 易（被 迫 或 清 盤 出 售
  current transaction between willing parties, other than in a forced              除 外）時 工 具 可 予 以 匯 兌 的 金 額 記 賬 。
  or liquidation sale.


  The fair value of contingent consideration included in the financial             計入按公允價值計入損益的金融資產
  assets at fair value through profit or loss is measured using the                的或然代價的公允價值以重大不可觀
  valuation technique of the discounted cash flow model using                      察市場輸入數據使用貼現現金流量模
  significant unobservable market inputs.                                          型估值技術計量 。


  Set out below is a summary of significant unobservable inputs                    下 表 概 列2025年6月30日 及2024年12月
  to the valuation of financial instruments as at June 30, 2025 and                31日 金 融 工 具 估 值 的 重 大 不 可 觀 察 輸
